}
=============== Main.java
package restaurantrush;

import java.io.FileInputStream;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.io.PrintStream;
import java.util.NoSuchElementException;

import restaurantrush.boundary.RestaurantCLI;
import restaurantrush.control.GameController;

/**
 * Entry point, and the one place that wires the command-line boundary to the controller. With no
 * arguments the manager types the choices. With "--input choices.txt" the choices are read from a
 * file instead (and echoed), which replays a recorded run.
 */
public final class Main {
    private Main() {
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        if (args.length == 0) {
            play(System.in, System.out, false);
        } else if (args.length == 2 && args[0].equals("--input")) {
            try (InputStream choices = new FileInputStream(args[1])) {
                play(choices, System.out, true);
            } catch (IOException unreadable) {
                System.err.println("Cannot read the choices file '" + args[1] + "': " + unreadable.getMessage());
                System.exit(1);
            }
        } else {
            System.err.println("Usage: java -cp out restaurantrush.Main [--input choices.txt]");
            System.exit(2);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Plays one game reading the manager's choices from the given stream. If the input ends before
     * the game does, the run stops with a message. echoInput repeats each line read, so a run fed
     * from a file reads like a transcript.
     */
    public static void play(InputStream in, PrintStream out, boolean echoInput) {
        RestaurantCLI cli = new RestaurantCLI(in, out, echoInput);
        try {
            new GameController(cli).startGame();
        } catch (NoSuchElementException inputEnded) {
            cli.displayInputEnded();
        }
    }
}

18	/**
19	 * The command-line boundary. It prints the game state and reads the manager's choices as text;
20	 * it contains no game rules (the staff and domain objects decide whether a choice is legal) and
21	 * does not know the controller: the controller calls it, and Main wires the two together.
22	 */
23	public class RestaurantCLI {
24	    private final Scanner input;
25	    private final PrintStream out;
26	    private final boolean echoInput;
27	
28	    /** echoInput repeats each line read, so a run fed from a file (stdin) reads like a transcript. */
29	    public RestaurantCLI(InputStream in, PrintStream out, boolean echoInput) {
30	        this.input = new Scanner(in);
31	        this.out = out;
32	        this.echoInput = echoInput;
33	    }
34	
35	    public void displayIntro(int maxTurns) {
36	        out.println("SC2002 Restaurant Rush - Stage 3");
37	        out.println("You manage a restaurant for " + maxTurns + " turns. Each turn the Waiter does one");
38	        out.println("task (SEAT, TAKE_ORDER or SERVE), the Host can also SEAT one queued customer, and the Chef");
39	        out.println("advances one order by one unit (COOK). Once per game you may switch Happy Hour on.");
40	        out.println("Press Enter to WAIT. Goal: at least " + Restaurant.REQUIRED_PAID_CUSTOMERS + " paid customers, revenue of at least "
41	                + Money.format(Restaurant.REQUIRED_REVENUE_CENTS) + ", average satisfaction of at least "
42	                + Restaurant.REQUIRED_AVERAGE_SATISFACTION + ".");
43	    }
44	
45	    /** Shown when the input ends before the game does. */
46	    public void displayInputEnded() {
47	        out.println();
48	        out.println("The input ended before the game finished, so the run stops here.");
49	    }
50	
51	    public String readHostChoice() {
52	        return readLine("Host    [SEAT <#> | WAIT]: ");
53	    }
54	
55	    public String readWaiterChoice() {
56	        return readLine("Waiter  [SEAT <#> | TAKE_ORDER <#> | SERVE <#> | WAIT]: ");
57	    }
58	
59	    public String readChefChoice() {
60	        return readLine("Chef    [COOK <#> | WAIT]: ");
61	    }
62	
63	    /** Asks whether to switch Happy Hour on now. Blank means no. */
64	    public boolean readHappyHourChoice() {
65	        while (true) {
66	            String answer = readLine("Happy Hour [y = switch on for this turn and the next | n or Enter = not now]: ")
67	                    .trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
68	            if (answer.isEmpty() || answer.equals("n") || answer.equals("no")) {
69	                return false;
70	            }
71	            if (answer.equals("y") || answer.equals("yes")) {
72	                return true;
73	            }
74	            displayError("'" + answer + "' is not y or n");
75	        }
76	    }
77	
78	    public void displayTurnStatus(Restaurant restaurant, int turn, int maxTurns) {
79	        out.println();
80	        out.println("=================== TURN " + turn + " of " + maxTurns + " ===================");
81	        out.println("Revenue: " + Money.format(restaurant.getRevenueCents())
82	                + " | Average satisfaction of paid customers: " + averageText(restaurant)
83	                + " | Served: " + restaurant.getServedCustomerCount()
84	                + " | Unhappy departures: " + restaurant.getUnhappyDepartureCount());
85	        out.println("Happy Hour: " + happyHourText(restaurant.getHappyHour()));
86	        out.println("Waiting queue: " + queueText(restaurant));
87	        out.println("Tables: " + tablesText(restaurant));
88	        out.println("Customers:");
89	        if (restaurant.getCustomers().isEmpty()) {
90	            out.println("  (nobody has arrived yet)");
91	        }
92	        for (Customer customer : restaurant.getCustomers()) {
93	            out.println(String.format(Locale.ROOT, "  %-4s %-8s satisfaction %3d  %-12s  order: %s",
94	                    "#" + customer.getId(), customer.getTypeLabel(), customer.getSatisfaction(), customer.getStatus(),
95	                    orderText(customer)));
96	        }
97	        out.println("Host tasks:   " + hostTasksText(restaurant));
98	        out.println("Waiter tasks: " + waiterTasksText(restaurant));
99	        out.println("Chef tasks:   " + chefTasksText(restaurant));
100	    }
101	
102	    public void displayMessage(String message) {
103	        out.println("  * " + message);
104	    }
105	
106	    public void displayError(String message) {
107	        String reason = message.endsWith(".") ? message.substring(0, message.length() - 1) : message;
108	        out.println("  ! Not possible: " + reason + ". Choose again.");
109	    }
110	
111	    public void displayResult(boolean won, Restaurant restaurant, int turnsUsed) {
112	        out.println();
113	        out.println("=================== GAME OVER ===================");
114	        out.println(won ? "VICTORY" : "DEFEAT");
115	        out.println("Paid customers: " + restaurant.getPaidCustomerCount() + " (need "
116	                + Restaurant.REQUIRED_PAID_CUSTOMERS + ")");
117	        out.println("Revenue: " + Money.format(restaurant.getRevenueCents()) + " (need "
118	                + Money.format(Restaurant.REQUIRED_REVENUE_CENTS) + ")");
119	        out.println("Average satisfaction of paid customers: " + averageText(restaurant) + " (need "
120	                + Restaurant.REQUIRED_AVERAGE_SATISFACTION + ")");
121	        out.println("Customers served: " + restaurant.getServedCustomerCount()
122	                + " | Unhappy departures: " + restaurant.getUnhappyDepartureCount()
123	                + " | Turns used: " + turnsUsed);
124	    }
125	
126	    private String readLine(String prompt) {
127	        out.print(prompt);
128	        out.flush();
129	        String line = input.nextLine();
130	        if (echoInput) {
131	            out.println(line);
132	        }
133	        return line;
134	    }
135	
136	    private static String averageText(Restaurant restaurant) {
137	        if (restaurant.getPaidCustomerCount() == 0) {
138	            return "N/A";
139	        }
140	        return String.format(Locale.ROOT, "%.1f", restaurant.getAverageSatisfaction());
141	    }
142	
143	    private static String happyHourText(HappyHour happyHour) {
144	        if (happyHour.isActive()) {
145	            return "ACTIVE, " + happyHour.getRemainingTurns() + " turn(s) left including this one";
146	        }
147	        return happyHour.isAvailable() ? "available (switch on once per game)" : "used";
148	    }
149	
150	    private static String queueText(Restaurant restaurant) {
151	        if (restaurant.getWaitingQueue().isEmpty()) {
152	            return "empty";
153	        }
154	        List<String> entries = new ArrayList<>();
155	        for (Customer customer : restaurant.getWaitingQueue()) {
156	            entries.add("#" + customer.getId() + " " + customer.getTypeLabel() + " (satisfaction " + customer.getSatisfaction() + ")");
157	        }
158	        return String.join(" -> ", entries);
159	    }
160	
161	    private static String tablesText(Restaurant restaurant) {
162	        List<String> entries = new ArrayList<>();
163	        for (Table table : restaurant.getTables()) {
164	            Customer occupant = table.getOccupied();
165	            entries.add("Table " + table.getTableId() + ": "
166	                    + (occupant == null ? "free"
167	                            : "#" + occupant.getId() + " " + occupant.getTypeLabel() + " (" + occupant.getStatus() + ")"));
168	        }
169	        return String.join(" | ", entries);
170	    }
171	
172	    private static String orderText(Customer customer) {
173	        Order order = customer.getOrder();
174	        if (order == null) {
175	            return "-";
176	        }
177	        return order.getItem().getName() + " " + order.getCompletedUnits() + "/"
178	                + order.getItem().getPreparationUnits() + " units, " + order.getStatus()
179	                + ", " + Money.format(order.getBillCents());
180	    }
181	
182	    private static String hostTasksText(Restaurant restaurant) {
183	        List<Customer> seatable = restaurant.getSeatableCustomers();
184	        return seatable.isEmpty() ? "none available (WAIT)" : "SEAT " + ids(seatable);
185	    }
186	
187	    private static String waiterTasksText(Restaurant restaurant) {
188	        List<String> tasks = new ArrayList<>();
189	        List<Customer> seatable = restaurant.getSeatableCustomers();
190	        if (!seatable.isEmpty()) {
191	            tasks.add("SEAT " + ids(seatable));
192	        }
193	        List<Customer> toOrder = restaurant.getCustomersWithStatus(CustomerStatus.SEATED);
194	        if (!toOrder.isEmpty()) {
195	            tasks.add("TAKE_ORDER " + ids(toOrder));
196	        }
197	        List<Customer> toServe = restaurant.getCustomersToServe();
198	        if (!toServe.isEmpty()) {
199	            tasks.add("SERVE " + ids(toServe));
200	        }
201	        return tasks.isEmpty() ? "none available (WAIT)" : String.join("; ", tasks);
202	    }
203	
204	    private static String chefTasksText(Restaurant restaurant) {
205	        List<Customer> toCook = restaurant.getCustomersToCook();
206	        return toCook.isEmpty() ? "none available (WAIT)" : "COOK " + ids(toCook);
207	    }
208	
209	    private static String ids(List<Customer> customers) {
210	        List<String> numbers = new ArrayList<>();
211	        for (Customer customer : customers) {
212	            numbers.add("#" + customer.getId());
213	        }
214	        return String.join(" ", numbers);
215	    }
216	}

1	# Test notes
2	
3	Run every command from the repository root with JDK 17 or newer. Build first:
4	
5	```
6	javac --release 17 -d out -sourcepath src src/restaurantrush/Main.java
7	```
8	
9	The recorded runs in `runs/` belong to the stage they are checked out with. This folder holds the Stage 3 runs; the Stage 1 and Stage 2 runs are in the `stage-1` and `stage-2` tags (`git checkout stage-1`).
10	
11	## Stage 3 (tag `stage-3`)
12	
13	### Automated checks
14	
15	```
16	javac --release 17 -d out -sourcepath src test/restaurantrush/GameTests.java
17	java -cp out restaurantrush.GameTests
18	```
19	
20	Expected: `194 checks passed, 0 failed`. The checks cover:
21	
22	- **Stage 1 and 2 rules that must still hold**: order status rules, the Regular's loss of 8, the turn guards, tables, payment, abandonment, loss per waiting turn for Regular, VIP and Critic (8, 5, 12), the VIP's 10% discount, the locked bill, the Critic's food and late-service penalty, the six arrivals (including a VIP and a Critic), and that `GameController`, `StaffActions` and `ManagerCommand` contain no type checks. The food-rule checks that assume three dishes build that three-dish menu explicitly, because the Stage 3 menu has five entries;
23	- **ComboMeal**: Salad + Burger $21.60 and 2 units, Salad + Pasta $24.30 and 3 units, Burger + Pasta $29.70 and 3 units; a combo holds its two component items and is a `MenuItem`; a 3-unit combo is READY only after 3 units; the menu has five entries (the three dishes and two combos built from the menu's own dishes); Regular and VIP choose by `customerId mod 5`, the Critic takes the Salad + Pasta combo;
24	- **Happy Hour**: lasts exactly two turns, can be switched on once per game (a second activation is refused and does not restart it), VIP Burger $10.80, Regular Pasta $14.40, Critic's combo $19.44, no discount when off; an order placed before Happy Hour keeps its price, and an order taken during it keeps the discount after it ends; waiting customers recover 15 (capped at 100), served customers and customers who have left do not;
25	- **Host**: seats a queued customer, refuses an occupied table or a customer who is not waiting without changing anything, and the Waiter can still seat;
26	- **Stage 3 clean-up** (no behaviour change): the waiting queue is read-only and keeps arrival order; `seatFromQueue` removes a customer from it only after the Waiter or Host accepted the seating, and a rejected seating leaves the customer in the queue; `Restaurant` reports who can be seated, cooked for or served (and nobody can be seated while both tables are occupied); `hasMetObjective()` is false until 4 customers have paid and true at 4 paid, $72.00 and satisfaction 100; `RestaurantCLI` does not use the controller or recompute the staff-task rules; `Order` has no static mutable state. The replays of the recorded runs and an invalid-input session print the same transcripts as before the clean-up;
27	- **The recorded runs below**: results, Happy Hour offered only until used (and offered again after being declined), the Host seating while the Waiter takes the order in the same turn, rejected choices explained and followed by another prompt in the same turn.
28	- **Command selection regression**: with one READY order and one PREPARING order, `SERVE` and `COOK` without a customer number select only the customer eligible for that task. Explicit requests to serve an unfinished order or cook a READY order are rejected without consuming the task; two READY orders still require a customer number for `SERVE`.
29

52	| 2 | | WAIT | SERVE 1 | WAIT | Last Happy Hour turn: the VIP recovers 95 to 100. READY to SERVED on a later turn |

56	| 6 | | WAIT | SERVE 3 (rejected), SERVE 2 | WAIT | "There is no customer #3" |
57	| 7 | | SEAT 3 | TAKE_ORDER 3 | COOK 3 | #3 arrives and orders the Salad + Burger combo ($21.60, 2 units) |
58	| 8 | | WAIT | WAIT | COOK 3 | Cashier collects $18.00 from #2; combo READY after 2 units |
59	| 9 | | WAIT | SERVE 3 | WAIT | |
60	| 10 | | SEAT 4 | TAKE_ORDER 4 | COOK 4 | #4 (Critic) arrives and orders the Salad + Pasta combo ($24.30, 3 units) |
61	| 11 | | WAIT | WAIT | COOK 4 | Cashier collects $21.60 from #3 |
62	| 12 | | WAIT | WAIT | COOK 4 | combo READY after 3 units |
63	| 13 | | SEAT 5 | SERVE 4 | WAIT | Manager choice: the Waiter serves the Critic (3 turns after ordering: -10, satisfaction 54) before taking #5's order |

70	Choices that change the outcome (checked by replaying the run with a different choice):
71	
72	- **Happy Hour.** Never switching it on gives $86.40 revenue and average satisfaction 80.2 instead of $83.70 and 81.2: Happy Hour costs $2.70 of revenue and buys one satisfaction point on average here, which matters when the average is close to 60.
73	- **Turn 13.** The run serves the Critic first (satisfaction 54 at payment). Taking #5's order first and serving the Critic in turn 14 costs the Critic a further 12, so it pays with 42 and the average falls from 81.2 to 78.8 (still a victory, with the same revenue).
74	
75	### Reproducible defeat run

78	java -cp out restaurantrush.Main --input runs/defeat.txt
79	```
80	
81	The manager switches Happy Hour on in turn 1, serves the VIP through to payment, and then only chooses WAIT. Expected ending: `DEFEAT`, 1 paid customer, revenue $10.80, average satisfaction 100.0, 1 served, 2 unhappy departures (customer 2, a Regular, leaves at the end of turn 16; customer 4, the Critic, at the end of turn 18), 18 turns used. A perfectly happy customer is not enough: all three targets must be met.
82	
83	### Invalid input

85	`runs/victory.txt` contains a rejected Host choice (turn 1, `SEAT 5` before customer 5 exists) and a rejected Waiter choice (turn 6, `SERVE 3`). Each prints the reason and asks again in the same turn. `GameTests` checks the messages and that the matching prompt follows. A Happy Hour answer other than `y` or `n` is rejected and asked again (checked by an inline input in `GameTests`).

----- STAGE-2 GameController
package restaurantrush.control;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collection;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.function.BiConsumer;
import java.util.function.Consumer;
import java.util.function.Supplier;

import restaurantrush.boundary.RestaurantCLI;
import restaurantrush.entity.Customer;
import restaurantrush.entity.CustomerStatus;
import restaurantrush.entity.MenuItem;
import restaurantrush.entity.Money;
import restaurantrush.entity.Order;
import restaurantrush.entity.Restaurant;
import restaurantrush.entity.Table;

/**
 * Runs the game: 18 turns of six steps each (arrivals, manager choices, kitchen and serving,
 * eating and payment, waiting and abandonment, result). It owns the turn counter and decides
 * whether a choice is legal; the CLI only collects choices and displays state.
 */
public class GameController {
    public static final int REQUIRED_PAID_CUSTOMERS = 4;
    public static final int REQUIRED_REVENUE_CENTS = 4500;
    public static final int REQUIRED_AVERAGE_SATISFACTION = 60;

    private static final int MAX_TURNS = 18;

    private final Restaurant restaurant;
    private final ArrivalSchedule schedule;
    private int currentTurn;
    private final int maxTurns;
    private final RestaurantCLI cli;

    public GameController(RestaurantCLI cli) {
        this.cli = cli;
        this.restaurant = new Restaurant();
        this.schedule = new ArrivalSchedule();
        this.maxTurns = MAX_TURNS;
    }

    public int getCurrentTurn() {
        return currentTurn;
    }

    public int getMaxTurns() {
        return maxTurns;
    }

    /** Plays all turns (there is no early ending), then reports victory or defeat. */
    public void startGame() {
        while (currentTurn < maxTurns) {
            currentTurn++;
            executeTurn();
        }
        cli.displayResult(evaluateGameResult(), restaurant);
    }

    private void executeTurn() {
        processScheduledArrival();
        cli.displayTurnStatus(restaurant);
        coordinateStaffActions();
        coordinatePaymentProcessing();
        processEndOfTurnUpdates();
    }

    /** Step 1: the schedule decides who arrives at the start of turns 1, 4, 7, 10, 13 and 16. */
    private void processScheduledArrival() {
        if (schedule.isArrivalTurn(currentTurn)) {
            restaurant.registerArrival(schedule.createArrival(currentTurn));
        }
    }

    /** Steps 2 and 3: the Waiter acts first, then the Chef. A rejected choice does not use up the task. */
    private void coordinateStaffActions() {
        chooseUntilAccepted(cli::readWaiterChoice, this::performWaiterTask);
        chooseUntilAccepted(cli::readChefChoice, this::performChefTask);
    }

    private void chooseUntilAccepted(Supplier<String> reader, Consumer<String> task) {
        while (true) {
            String choice = reader.get();
            try {
                task.accept(choice);
                return;
            } catch (IllegalArgumentException | IllegalStateException rejected) {
                cli.displayError(rejected.getMessage());
            }
        }
    }

    private void performWaiterTask(String choice) {
        Command command = Command.parse(choice);
        switch (command.verb) {
            case "WAIT" -> { }
            case "SEAT" -> seatQueuedCustomer(command, restaurant.getWaiter()::seatCustomer, "Waiter");
            case "TAKE_ORDER", "ORDER", "TAKE" -> takeOrder(command);
            case "SERVE" -> serve(command);
            default -> throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Unknown Waiter command '" + command.verb + "'. Use SEAT, TAKE_ORDER, SERVE or WAIT.");
        }
    }

    private void performChefTask(String choice) {
        Command command = Command.parse(choice);
        switch (command.verb) {
            case "WAIT" -> { }
            case "COOK", "PREPARE", "ADVANCE" -> prepare(command);
            default -> throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Unknown Chef command '" + command.verb + "'. Use COOK or WAIT.");
        }
    }

    private void seatQueuedCustomer(Command command, BiConsumer<Customer, Table> seater, String actor) {
        Customer customer = select(command, new ArrayList<>(restaurant.getWaitingQueue()),
                "nobody is in the waiting queue", "only a customer in the waiting queue can be seated");
        Table table = restaurant.findAvailableTable();
        if (table == null) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Both tables are occupied; a table frees up when its customer pays or leaves");
        }
        seater.accept(customer, table);
        restaurant.getWaitingQueue().remove(customer);
        cli.displayMessage(actor + " seats " + describe(customer) + " at Table " + table.getTableId() + ".");
    }

    private void takeOrder(Command command) {
        Customer customer = select(command, customersWithStatus(CustomerStatus.SEATED),
                "no seated customer is waiting to order", "only a seated customer can order");
        Order order = restaurant.getWaiter().takeOrder(customer, restaurant.getMenu(), currentTurn);
        MenuItem item = order.getItem();
        cli.displayMessage("Waiter takes the order of " + describe(customer) + ". They choose "
                + item.getName() + " (list price " + Money.format(item.getPriceCents()) + ", "
                + item.getPreparationUnits() + " preparation unit(s)). Bill locked at "
                + Money.format(order.getBillCents()) + ".");
    }

    private void serve(Command command) {
        Customer customer = select(command, customersWithStatus(CustomerStatus.ORDERED),
                "no customer has an order to serve", "only a customer who has ordered can be served");
        restaurant.getWaiter().serveOrder(customer, currentTurn);
        cli.displayMessage("Waiter serves the " + customer.getOrder().getItem().getName() + " to "
                + describe(customer) + ".");
    }

    private void prepare(Command command) {
        Customer customer = select(command, customersWithStatus(CustomerStatus.ORDERED),
                "no order has been taken yet", "only a customer who has ordered has food to prepare");
        Order order = customer.getOrder();
        restaurant.getChef().prepare(order);
        cli.displayMessage("Chef works on the " + order.getItem().getName() + " for " + describe(customer)
                + ": " + order.getCompletedUnits() + "/" + order.getItem().getPreparationUnits()
                + " units, order is " + order.getStatus() + ".");
    }

    /** Step 4: served customers finish eating, then the Cashier charges at most one READY_TO_PAY customer. */
    private void coordinatePaymentProcessing() {
        for (Customer customer : restaurant.getCustomers()) {
            if (customer.canFinishEating(currentTurn)) {
                customer.markReadyToPay(currentTurn);
                cli.displayMessage(describe(customer) + " finishes eating and is ready to pay.");
            }
        }
        Customer payer = restaurant.findOldestEligiblePayer(currentTurn);
        if (payer != null) {
            int bill = payer.getOrder().getBillCents();
            restaurant.getCashier().processPayment(payer, restaurant);
            cli.displayMessage("Cashier collects " + Money.format(bill) + " from " + describe(payer)
                    + " (satisfaction " + payer.getSatisfaction() + "). The table is free again.");
        }
    }

    /** Step 5: customers who are still waiting lose satisfaction; at 0 they leave without paying. */
    private void processEndOfTurnUpdates() {
        for (Customer customer : restaurant.getCustomers()) {
            customer.reduceSatisfaction();
            if (customer.shouldAbandon()) {
                restaurant.handleAbandonment(customer);
                cli.displayMessage(describe(customer)
                        + " runs out of patience and leaves without paying. Any unfinished order is cancelled.");
            }
        }
    }

    /** Step 6: victory needs all three targets. */
    private boolean evaluateGameResult() {
        return restaurant.getPaidCustomerCount() >= REQUIRED_PAID_CUSTOMERS
                && restaurant.getRevenueCents() >= REQUIRED_REVENUE_CENTS
                && restaurant.getAverageSatisfaction() >= REQUIRED_AVERAGE_SATISFACTION;
    }

    /** Resolves the customer a command refers to, or explains why it cannot. */
    private Customer select(Command command, List<Customer> candidates, String noCandidates, String ineligible) {
        if (command.customerId == null) {
            if (candidates.isEmpty()) {
                throw new IllegalStateException("Nothing to do: " + noCandidates);
            }
            if (candidates.size() > 1) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException("Several customers qualify (" + idList(candidates)
                        + "); add a customer number, for example " + command.verb + " " + candidates.get(0).getId());
            }
            return candidates.get(0);
        }
        for (Customer candidate : candidates) {
            if (candidate.getId() == command.customerId) {
                return candidate;
            }
        }
        for (Customer customer : restaurant.getCustomers()) {
            if (customer.getId() == command.customerId) {
                throw new IllegalStateException(describe(customer) + " is " + customer.getStatus()
                        + ": " + ineligible);
            }
        }
        throw new IllegalArgumentException("There is no customer #" + command.customerId);
    }

    private List<Customer> customersWithStatus(CustomerStatus status) {
        List<Customer> matching = new ArrayList<>();
        for (Customer customer : restaurant.getCustomers()) {
            if (customer.getStatus() == status) {
                matching.add(customer);
            }
        }
        return matching;
    }

    private static String describe(Customer customer) {
        return "Customer #" + customer.getId() + " (" + customer.getTypeLabel() + ")";
    }

    private static String idList(Collection<Customer> customers) {
        StringBuilder ids = new StringBuilder();
        for (Customer customer : customers) {
            ids.append(ids.length() == 0 ? "#" : ", #").append(customer.getId());
        }
        return ids.toString();
    }

    /** A manager command: a verb and an optional customer number. A blank line means WAIT. */
    private static final class Command {
        private final String verb;
        private final Integer customerId;

        private Command(String verb, Integer customerId) {
            this.verb = verb;
            this.customerId = customerId;
        }

        private static Command parse(String raw) {
            String text = raw == null ? "" : raw.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
            if (text.isEmpty()) {
                return new Command("WAIT", null);
            }
            String[] words = text.split("\\s+");
            if (words.length > 2) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException("Expected 'COMMAND [customer number]' but got '" + raw.trim() + "'");
            }
            Integer id = null;
            if (words.length == 2) {
                String number = words[1].startsWith("#") ? words[1].substring(1) : words[1];
                try {
                    id = Integer.valueOf(number);
                } catch (NumberFormatException notANumber) {
                    throw new IllegalArgumentException("'" + words[1] + "' is not a customer number");
                }
            }
            return new Command(words[0], id);
        }
    }
}

430	' ---------- annotations ----------
431	note top of Restaurant
432	  <b>COMPOSITION</b>
433	  Restaurant creates and owns its Tables, Menu,
434	  HappyHour and staff; they exist only as part of
435	  one Restaurant. Customers are aggregated:
436	  they arrive and leave. The waitingQueue and
437	  customers links both point at Customer.
438	end note
439	
440	note right of StaffActions
441	  <b>RESPONSIBILITY SPLIT</b>
442	  GameController only sequences the six steps.
443	  ManagerCommand reads one typed choice.
444	  StaffActions carries it out with the Host, Waiter
445	  or Chef and returns the sentence to show; the
446	  staff and domain objects reject what is illegal.
447	  Restaurant answers what each staff member can do
448	  now (getSeatableCustomers(), getCustomersToServe(),
449	  getCustomersToCook()), so RestaurantCLI only prints.
450	end note
451	
452	note bottom of Order
453	  <b>ENCAPSULATION</b>
454	  status is private and changes only through
455	  advancePreparation(), markServed(), markPaid(),
456	  cancel(); illegal transitions are rejected.
457	  Legal: PLACED → PREPARING → READY → SERVED → PAID
458	  (PLACED → READY directly for a 1-unit dish);
459	  PLACED / PREPARING / READY → CANCELLED if the
460	  customer leaves.
461	  billCents is set once in the constructor with no
462	  setter: the price is locked when ordered.
463	end note
464	
465	note bottom of CustomerStatus
466	  <b>CUSTOMER LIFECYCLE</b>
467	  WAITING → SEATED → ORDERED → SERVED →
468	  READY_TO_PAY → PAID; WAITING / SEATED / ORDERED
469	  → ABANDONED when satisfaction reaches 0.
470	  Served, eating and READY_TO_PAY customers
471	  lose no satisfaction.
472	end note
473	
474	note left of Customer
475	  <b>POLYMORPHISM</b>
476	  getSatisfactionLoss(), calculateBillCents(),
477	  chooseMenuItem() and onServed() are overridden.
478	  GameController and Waiter call them on Customer
479	  and never check the type.
480	  Loss per waiting turn: Regular 8, VIP 5, Critic 12.
481	  <b>ENCAPSULATION</b>
482	  satisfaction is private; subclasses change it only
483	  through adjustSatisfaction() (clamped 0..100).
484	  <b>TURN GUARDS</b>
485	  canFinishEating(turn): only after the serving turn.
486	  canPay(turn): only after the READY_TO_PAY turn.
487	end note
488	
489	note right of Waiter
490	  <b>BILLING</b>
491	  takeOrder(): bill = happyHour.applyDiscount(
492	    customer.calculateBillCents(price))
493	  VIP discount first, then Happy Hour; locked in
494	  the new Order. VIP Burger: 1500, 1350, 1080 cents.
495	end note
496	
497	note bottom of Restaurant
498	  <b>ENCAPSULATION</b>
499	  getWaitingQueue() is read-only; a customer leaves the
500	  queue only through seatFromQueue(), after the Waiter
501	  or Host has accepted the seating.
502	end note
503	
504	note right of HappyHour
505	  <b>HAPPY HOUR</b>
506	  Once per game, at turn start; active for that turn
507	  and the next. 20% off orders taken; +15 satisfaction
508	  (max 100) for customers waiting for a table or food.
509	end note
510	
511	note right of CriticCustomer
512	  <b>CRITIC RULE</b>
513	  Loses 12 per waiting turn. Always orders the most
514	  expensive menu item (Salad + Pasta, $24.30, 3 units).
515	  Served more than 2 turns after ordering: loses an
516	  extra 10 (onServed). A 3-unit combo cannot be served
517	  that fast, so this Critic always loses the extra 10.
518	end note
519	
520	note bottom of ComboMeal
521	  <b>COMBO</b>
522	  Hollow-triangle line: a ComboMeal is a MenuItem. Orange
523	  diamond line: it holds 2 MenuItem components (a MenuItem
524	  can be a component of 0..* combos).
525	  Stage 3 menu =Salad, Burger, Pasta + 2 ComboMeals
526	  (5 entries), registered once at game setup.
527	  Customer.chooseMenuItem(menu) picks from it.
528	  price = (a.price + b.price) x 90%, rounded:
529	  (sumCents x 90 + 50) / 100; units = a.units + b.units.
530	  Salad + Burger $21.60, 2 units; Salad + Pasta $24.30,
531	  3 units. Choice rule: customerId % menu.size(), so
532	  indexes 3 and 4 are the combos.
533	end note
534	
535	legend left
536	  <b>KEY</b>
537	  <back:#CCEBD2>  green  </back> new in Stage 3
538	  <back:#FAE8A6>  yellow  </back> changed since Stage 2
539	  <back:#DCEBF7>  blue  </back> unchanged
540	  <b>Changed since Stage 2</b>
541	  Customer: + recoverSatisfaction(), getDisplayName();
542	    isAwaitingService() is now public
543	  Waiter: takeOrder(...) also takes HappyHour
544	  MenuItem: no longer final (ComboMeal extends it)
545	  Restaurant: + host, happyHour, two ComboMeals on the
546	    menu; read-only waitingQueue with seatFromQueue(),
547	    staff task queries, hasMetObjective()
548	  Order: orderId and its static counter removed
549	  GameController: Host and Happy Hour steps; command
550	    parsing and task execution moved to ManagerCommand
551	    and StaffActions
552	  RestaurantCLI: Host and Happy Hour prompts; no longer
553	    references GameController (Main wires them)
554	  <b>Complete:</b> every attribute, constructor and
555	  operation in the Stage 3 code is listed (underlined =
556	  static; private helpers included).
557	end legend
558	@enduml
559

7	' Turn 4 also has the base-setting arrival of customer 2, seated by the Host.

49	note over M, rest
50	  <b>Stage 3 | base setting (18 turns, 2 tables)</b>
51	  <b>Start:</b> turn 1, restaurant empty, 2 free tables. A VIP arrives
52	  (satisfaction 100). Burger = $15.00, 1 preparation unit.
53	  <b>Manager choice:</b> turn on Happy Hour at turn 1 or 2 (or never).
54	  <b>Outcome:</b> the VIP is PAID on turn 5, bill locked at <b>$10.80</b>,
55	  satisfaction <b>95</b>. If never activated: bill $13.50, satisfaction 90.
56	  <b>Host:</b> left at WAIT in turns 1-3 (the Waiter seats the VIP); in turn 4
57	  it seats customer 2, who is not traced further.
58	end note
59	
60	M -> main : run the game
61	main -> cli : <<create>> RestaurantCLI(in, out, echoInput)
62	main -> game : <<create>> GameController(cli)
63	note right of game : The constructor also creates the Restaurant, the ArrivalSchedule and StaffActions.\nStaffActions takes the HappyHour, the Menu or a staff member from the Restaurant\ngetters (getHappyHour(), getMenu(), getWaiter() ...); those calls are not drawn.\nEvery choice is read with ManagerCommand.parse(choice) (drawn where a task is done)
64	main -> game : startGame()
65	game -> cli : displayIntro(18)
66	
67	loop for each turn 1..18 (turns 1-5 shown)
68	
69	  == Turn 1: VIP arrives, manager turns Happy Hour on, Waiter seats the VIP ==
70	
71	  game -> game : **1 Arrivals** processScheduledArrival()
72	  game -> sched : arrival = isArrivalTurn(1)
73	  opt arrival
74	    game -> sched : vip = createArrival(1)
75	    create entity "vip : VIPCustomer" as vip order 80
76	    sched --> vip : <<create>>
77	    note right : status WAITING,\nsatisfaction 100
78	    game -> rest : registerArrival(vip)
79	    note right : vip joins\nwaitingQueue (arrival order)
80	  end
81	  game -> cli : displayTurnStatus(restaurant, 1, 18)
82	
83	  game -> game : **Start of turn** processHappyHourActivation()
84	  game -> hh : available = isAvailable()
85	  opt available (once per game)
86	    game -> cli : choice = readHappyHourChoice()
87	    cli -> M : Activate Happy Hour? (y/n)
88	    M --> cli : y
89	    alt choice == y
90	      game -> hh : activate()
91	      note right : used = true, remainingTurns = 2\n(this turn and the next)
92	    else choice == n
93	      note over game, hh : Declined now: Happy Hour stays available, so it can\nstill be switched on in turn 2 (same outcome here).\nNever activated: bill $13.50, satisfaction 90
94	    end
95	  end
96	
97	  game -> game : applyHappyHourRecovery()
98	  game -> hh : active = isActive()
99	  opt active
100	    game -> hh : amount = getRecoveryAmount()
101	    loop for each customer where isAwaitingService()
102	      game -> vip : recoverSatisfaction(amount)
103	      note right : 100 stays 100 (capped)
104	    end
105	  end
106	
107	  game -> game : **2 Manager choices (Host)** coordinateHostAction()
108	  game -> cli : hostChoice = readHostChoice()
109	  cli -> M : Host: SEAT or WAIT?
110	  M --> cli : WAIT
111	  note right of game : The Host seats one queued customer on top of the Waiter's\ntask. WAIT here: the Waiter seats the VIP instead.
112	
113	  game -> game : **2-3 Manager choices and staff actions** coordinateStaffActions()
114	  note over game, cli : Steps 3-4 have nothing to do until a dish is READY\nor a customer is served (drawn from turn 3)
115	  note over game, cli : With several candidates the manager also picks which customer\nor order goes first; here there is only one candidate
116	  game -> cli : choice = readWaiterChoice()
117	  cli -> M : Waiter: SEAT, TAKE_ORDER, SERVE or WAIT?
118	  M --> cli : SEAT
119	  game -> cmd : cmd = parse(choice)
120	  game -> staff : message = waiter(cmd, 1)
121	  opt cmd.getVerb() == SEAT
122	    staff -> rest : queue = getWaitingQueue()
123	    staff -> cmd : vip = select(queue, customers, ...)
124	    note right : no number typed and one candidate:\nthe VIP is chosen
125	    staff -> rest : t1 = findAvailableTable()
126	    staff -> rest : seatFromQueue(vip, t1, waiter::seatCustomer)
127	    rest -> waiter : seatCustomer(vip, t1)
128	    waiter -> t1 : seat(vip)
129	    waiter -> vip : markSeated()
130	    note right : WAITING to SEATED
131	    note over rest : only after the Waiter accepted: vip leaves the waitingQueue
132	  end
133	  staff --> game : "Waiter seats Customer #1 (VIP) at Table 1."
134	  game -> cli : displayMessage(message)
135	  note over game, cli : Chef: manager picks WAIT (no order exists yet)
136	
137	  game -> game : **5 Waiting and abandonment** processEndOfTurnUpdates()
138	  game -> rest : customers = getCustomers()
139	  loop for each customer where isAwaitingService()
140	    game -> vip : reduceSatisfaction()
141	    vip -> vip : adjustSatisfaction(-getSatisfactionLoss())
142	    note right : polymorphism: loss per waiting turn\nRegular 8, VIP 5, Critic 12\n100 to 95
143	    game -> vip : leave = shouldAbandon()
144	    opt leave (satisfaction == 0)
145	      game -> rest : handleAbandonment(vip)
146	      note right : cancel order, free table, ABANDONED
147	    end
148	  end
149	  game -> hh : endTurn()
150	  note right : remainingTurns 2 to 1
151	
152	  newpage Restaurant Rush: Stage 3 sequence diagram (page 2 of 3)
153	  == Turn 2: Happy Hour recovery, Waiter takes the order (price locked), Chef finishes it ==
154	
155	  game -> game : applyHappyHourRecovery()
156	  game -> hh : active = isActive()
157	  loop for each customer where isAwaitingService()
158	    game -> vip : recoverSatisfaction(15)
159	    note right : 95 to 100 (capped at 100)
160	  end
161	
162	  note over game, cli : Host: manager picks WAIT (no queued customer)
163	  game -> game : **2-3 Manager choices and staff actions** coordinateStaffActions()
164	  game -> cli : choice = readWaiterChoice()
165	  cli -> M : Waiter: SEAT, TAKE_ORDER, SERVE or WAIT?
166	  M --> cli : TAKE_ORDER
167	  game -> cmd : cmd = parse(choice)
168	  game -> staff : message = waiter(cmd, 2)
169	  opt cmd.getVerb() == TAKE_ORDER
170	    staff -> rest : seated = getCustomersWithStatus(SEATED)
171	    staff -> cmd : vip = select(seated, customers, ...)
172	    staff -> waiter : order = takeOrder(vip, menu, hh, 2)
173	    waiter -> vip : item = chooseMenuItem(menu)
174	    note right : documented rule: Burger\n(1500 cents, 1 unit)
175	    waiter -> vip : bill = calculateBillCents(1500)
176	    note right : polymorphism: VIPCustomer\noverrides it, 1500 x 0.9 = 1350
177	    waiter -> hh : bill = applyDiscount(1350)
178	    note right : Happy Hour active: 1350 x 0.8 = 1080\n(declined: stays 1350)
179	    create entity "order : Order" as order order 100
180	    waiter --> order : <<create>> Order(item, 1080, 2)
181	    note right : billCents set once in the\nconstructor, no setter:\nprice locked at 1080
182	    waiter -> vip : assignOrder(order)
183	    note right : SEATED to ORDERED
184	  end
185	  staff --> game : "Waiter takes the order ... Bill locked at $10.80."
186	  game -> cli : displayMessage(message)
187	  game -> cli : chefChoice = readChefChoice()
188	  cli -> M : Chef: choose an order or WAIT?
189	  M --> cli : COOK 1 (the VIP's order)
190	  game -> cmd : cmd = parse(chefChoice)
191	  game -> staff : message = chef(cmd)
192	  opt cmd.getVerb() == COOK
193	    staff -> rest : unfinished = getCustomersToCook()
194	    staff -> cmd : vip = select(unfinished, customers, ...)
195	    staff -> vip : order = getOrder()
196	    staff -> chef : prepare(order)
197	    chef -> order : advancePreparation()
198	    note right : Burger 1/1 unit: PLACED to READY.\nWaiter already acted this turn:\nserved on turn 3 at the earliest
199	  end
200	  staff --> game : "Chef works on the Burger ... order is READY."
201	  game -> cli : displayMessage(message)
202	
203	  game -> game : **5 Waiting and abandonment** processEndOfTurnUpdates()
204	  loop for each customer where isAwaitingService()
205	    game -> vip : reduceSatisfaction()
206	    note right : ORDERED, not yet served: 100 to 95\n(abandonment check as in turn 1)
207	  end
208	  game -> hh : endTurn()
209	  note right : remainingTurns 1 to 0\nHappy Hour is over
210	
211	  == Turn 3: Waiter serves the dish that became READY on an earlier turn ==
212	
213	  note over game, cli : Host: manager picks WAIT (no queued customer)
214	  game -> game : **2-3 Manager choices and staff actions** coordinateStaffActions()
215	  loop until the Waiter choice is accepted
216	    game -> cli : choice = readWaiterChoice()
217	    cli -> M : Waiter: SEAT, TAKE_ORDER, SERVE or WAIT?
218	    M --> cli : SERVE
219	    game -> cmd : cmd = parse(choice)
220	    game -> staff : message = waiter(cmd, 3)
221	    opt cmd.getVerb() == SERVE
222	      staff -> rest : readyCustomers = getCustomersToServe()
223	      staff -> cmd : vip = select(readyCustomers, customers, ...)
224	      alt a READY customer is selected (finished on turn 2)
225	        staff -> waiter : serveOrder(vip, 3)
226	        waiter -> order : ready = isReady()
227	        note right : true: candidate selection requires READY;\nthe Waiter also checks defensively
228	        waiter -> order : markServed()
229	        note right : READY to SERVED
230	        waiter -> vip : markServed(3)
231	        note right : ORDERED to SERVED, servedTurn = 3
232	        waiter -> order : turnTaken = getTurnTaken()
233	        waiter -> vip : onServed(3 - turnTaken)
234	        note right : polymorphic hook: Critic loses 10 if served\nmore than 2 turns after ordering;\nVIP does nothing
235	        staff --> game : "Waiter serves the Burger to Customer #1 (VIP)."
236	        game -> cli : displayMessage(message)
237	      else invalid selection (for example, an unfinished order)
238	        cmd --> staff : throws "only a customer with a READY order can be served"
239	        staff --> game : the exception passes through
240	        game -> cli : displayError(reason)
241	        note right : staff task is not used up,\nthe manager chooses again
242	      end
243	    end
244	  end
245	  note over game, cli : Chef: manager picks WAIT
246	  note over game : **5** served customers lose no satisfaction,\nso the waiting loop has nothing to do
247	
248	  newpage Restaurant Rush: Stage 3 sequence diagram (page 3 of 3)
249	  == Turn 4: customer 2 arrives and the Host seats them; the VIP becomes READY_TO_PAY ==
250	
251	  game -> game : **1 Arrivals** processScheduledArrival()
252	  game -> sched : arrival = isArrivalTurn(4)
253	  opt arrival
254	    game -> sched : c2 = createArrival(4)
255	    game -> rest : registerArrival(c2)
256	    note right : customer 2 joins the waitingQueue\n(created as in turn 1; type set by the schedule)
257	  end
258	  game -> cli : displayTurnStatus(restaurant, 4, 18)
259	
260	  game -> game : **2 Manager choices (Host)** coordinateHostAction()
261	  game -> cli : hostChoice = readHostChoice()
262	  cli -> M : Host: SEAT or WAIT?
263	  M --> cli : SEAT
264	  game -> cmd : cmd = parse(hostChoice)
265	  game -> staff : message = host(cmd)
266	  opt cmd.getVerb() == SEAT
267	    staff -> rest : c2 = the only queued customer (select), t2 = findAvailableTable()
268	    staff -> rest : seatFromQueue(c2, t2, host::seatCustomer)
269	    rest -> host : seatCustomer(c2, t2)
270	    note right : Host.seatCustomer(c2, t2): markSeated() and Table.seat(c2), then Restaurant\nremoves c2 from the waitingQueue. The Waiter's task is not used
271	  end
272	  staff --> game : "Host seats Customer #2 (Regular) at Table 2."
273	  game -> cli : displayMessage(message)
274	  note over game, cli : Waiter and Chef: manager picks WAIT
275	
276	  game -> game : **4 Eating and payment** coordinatePaymentProcessing()
277	  game -> rest : customers = getCustomers()
278	  loop for each customer (the vip is shown)
279	    game -> vip : eaten = canFinishEating(4)
280	    opt eaten (SERVED and 4 > servedTurn 3)
281	      game -> vip : markReadyToPay(4)
282	      note right : SERVED to READY_TO_PAY, readyTurn = 4
283	    end
284	  end
285	  game -> rest : payer = findOldestEligiblePayer(4)
286	  rest -> vip : canPay(4)
287	  note over game, cashier : false (4 > readyTurn 4 does not hold), so payer is null\nand the Cashier does not charge yet
288	  note over game, rest : **5** customer 2 is seated and unserved, so it loses its per-turn loss;\nthe VIP (READY_TO_PAY) loses nothing
289	
290	  == Turn 5: Cashier collects the locked bill ==
291	
292	  note over game, cli : Host: WAIT. Waiter takes customer 2's order and the Chef starts it\n(not traced further: this trace follows the VIP)
293	  game -> game : **4 Eating and payment** coordinatePaymentProcessing()
294	  note over game, rest : nobody is SERVED, so nobody new finishes eating
295	  game -> rest : payer = findOldestEligiblePayer(5)
296	  rest -> vip : canPay(5)
297	  note over game, cashier : true (5 > readyTurn 4): payer is the vip
298	  opt payer is not null
299	    game -> cashier : processPayment(vip, restaurant)
300	    cashier -> vip : order = getOrder()
301	    cashier -> order : bill = getBillCents()
302	    note right : 1080: the locked price,\nnot recalculated
303	    cashier -> rest : recordPayment(bill)
304	    note right : revenue += $10.80\nvip counts as paid with satisfaction 95
305	    cashier -> vip : markPaid()
306	    note right : READY_TO_PAY to PAID
307	    cashier -> order : markPaid()
308	    note right : SERVED to PAID
309	    cashier -> rest : releaseTableOf(vip)
310	    rest -> t1 : release()
311	  end
312	
313	  ... turns 6 to 18 run the same six steps for the other customers ...
314	end
315	
316	game -> rest : **6 Result** won = hasMetObjective()
317	note right : paid customers >= 4 and revenue >= 4500\nand average satisfaction >= 60, from its own books\n(the game never ends early)
318	game -> cli : displayResult(won, restaurant, 18)
319	alt won
320	  cli -> M : Victory
321	else not won
322	  cli -> M : Defeat
323	end
324	@enduml

## 0093 User — 2026-11-08T18:47:06.637Z

Source record: 279; UUID: 6dc5f6fe-9c37-4727-a712-3248fa8eb1c5.

### Tool result: toolu_01E1fvSmrS2U2aYmhJ9PPKVD

1	                 SC2002 Restaurant Rush: Group Report
2	
3	                 SCEC (Group 5)
4	
5	1. Introduction
6	
7	Restaurant Rush is a turn-based restaurant game played in the terminal. The player is the manager. Each
8	turn the Waiter, Chef and (from Stage 3) Host can each do one task, so the manager has to decide which
9	customer or order gets attention first. We built it in three stages, each extending the saved version of
10	the previous one, and tagged each stage as its own runnable commit.
11	
12	Base setting.
13	
14	18 turns, 2 tables, one Waiter, one Chef, one Cashier, one Host (Stage 3). One customer arrives at the
15	start of turns 1, 4, 7, 10, 13 and 16.
16	
17	After turn 18 the player wins with at least 4 paid customers, revenue of at least $45.00 and an average
18	satisfaction of at least 60 among paying customers. The game never ends early.
19	
20	The code is in the GitHub repository, with one tag per stage (stage-1, stage-2, stage-3). The changes
21	between stages can be compared at stage-1...stage-2 and stage-2...stage-3.
22	
23	The diagrams are in docs/diagrams/ (PlantUML sources in docs/diagrams/src/), and docs/test-
24	notes.md lists the expected test and run results for each stage.
25	
26	How to run (JDK 17+, from the repository root, no other dependencies):
27	
28	javac --release 17 -d out -sourcepath src src/restaurantrush/Main.java
29	java -cp out restaurantrush.Main
30	java -cp out restaurantrush.Main --input runs/victory.txt
31	java -cp out restaurantrush.Main --input runs/defeat.txt

34	
35	seen without typing anything.

38	source at stage-1 has no customer subclasses, Host, ComboMeal or HappyHour, and stage-2 has none

67	2. Stage 1: the baseline game
68	
69	Stage 1 is a complete game with Regular customers only, and we wrote it as if Stages 2 and 3 did not
70	exist. The tag has no customer subclasses, no Host, no combo and no Happy Hour.
71	
72	We split the classes along the entity-control-boundary lines from the lectures:
73	
74	Layer    Class                   Responsibility
75	
76	Boundary RestaurantCLI           Prints the turn status and reads the manager's
77	                                 choices
78	
79	Control  GameController          Runs the 18 turns and the fixed order of steps inside
80	                                 a turn
81	
82	Entity   Restaurant              Owns the tables, staff, menu, customers and the
83	                                 waiting queue; records revenue
84	
85	Entity   Waiter, Chef, Cashier   One staff task each: seat/take order/serve, advance
86	                                 preparation, take payment
87	
88	Entity   Customer                Satisfaction, status and the turn guards
89	                                 (canFinishEating, canPay, shouldAbandon)
90	
91	Entity   Order                   Preparation progress and the order status
92	                                 transitions
93	
94	Entity   Table, MenuItem         A seat with at most one occupant; a dish with a
95	                                 price and preparation units
96	
97	Utility  Money, CustomerStatus,  Cents formatting and the two state enums
98	
99	         OrderStatus
100	
101	Turn order. 1 arrivals, 2-3 manager choices for the Waiter and Chef, 4 eating and payment, 5 waiting
102	and abandonment, 6 result after turn 18. The Waiter acts before the Chef, so a dish the Chef finishes
103	this turn can only be served next turn. A customer served on turn n becomes READY_TO_PAY on turn
104	n+1 and can pay on turn n+2.
105	
106	State protection. Order keeps its status private. It moves only through advancePreparation(),
107	markServed(), markPaid() and cancel(), and an illegal move throws.
108	
109	The legal path is PLACED  PREPARING  READY  SERVED  PAID, with PLACED  READY for a one-
110	unit dish and CANCELLED when a customer leaves. Customer never lets satisfaction fall below 0 and
111	checks its own status before every transition. Because these checks sit next to the state, an invalid
112	manager command cannot half-change the game.
113	
114	Money is stored in integer cents throughout, so a discount such as 10% then 20% off always gives the
115	same amount, rounded to the cent, instead of a floating-point rounding error.
116	
117	The Stage 1 class diagram is docs/diagrams/stage-1-class.pdf and is shown in the appendix.
118	
119	3. Stage 1  Stage 2: customer behaviour
120	
121	Stage 2 adds VIP and Critic customers. We made Customer abstract and let each subclass override the
122	behaviour that differs, so no other class has to check which type of customer it is dealing with.
123	
124	Type              Loss per Bill         Food choice               Reaction to service

138	                                                                  2 turns after ordering
139	
140	The varying behaviour sits behind four hooks on Customer:
141	
142	getSatisfactionLoss(), calculateBillCents(), chooseMenuItem(Menu) and
143	onServed(turnsWaited).
144	
145	Waiter.takeOrder calls chooseMenuItem and calculateBillCents, and serveOrder calls onServed,
146	all through a Customer reference; the end-of-turn loss goes through reduceSatisfaction(), which
147	calls getSatisfactionLoss(). Dynamic dispatch picks the right version each time. The controller
148	contains no instanceof, and GameTests checks that by scanning the source.

203	Effect on the design. Several files changed, but each change was small. Customer gained an abstract
204	contract, and every other edit is a signature change that follows from the bill being locked into Order.
205	Adding a fourth customer type now means one new subclass and one line in ArrivalSchedule.
206	4. Stage 2  Stage 3: Host, combo meals and Happy Hour
207	
208	Stage 3 adds a new staff role, a two-item combo and a once-per-game pricing policy. Most of the change
209	is new classes plus registration in Restaurant and GameController. The order, payment and customer
210	logic from Stage 2 was left alone.
211	
212	Host. Host.seatCustomer(customer, table) seats one queued customer per turn on top of the
213	Waiter's task. It reuses Table.seat and Customer.markSeated, so the same guards apply: an occupied
214	table or a customer who is not WAITING is refused and nothing changes.
215	
216	ComboMeal (composition). ComboMeal extends MenuItem and holds final references to two existing
217	MenuItems. Its price is 90% of their sum, rounded to the nearest cent, and its preparation units are the
218	sum. Because it is a MenuItem, Waiter, Chef, Cashier, Menu and Order need no changes to handle a
219	combo. The only edit needed was removing final from MenuItem.
220	
221	With the two combos the menu has five entries: Salad ($9.00, 1 unit), Burger ($15.00, 1 unit), Pasta
222	($18.00, 2 units), Salad + Burger ($21.60, 2 units) and Salad + Pasta ($24.30, 3 units). The combo values
223	match the reference values in section 7.
224	
225	HappyHour (policy). It can be switched on once, at the start of a turn, and lasts that turn and the next.
226	While active, new orders get 20% off and customers still waiting for a table, an order or their food
227	recover 15 satisfaction (capped at 100). Waiter.takeOrder applies it after the customer's own pricing
228	and stores the result in Order, so a VIP Burger is $15.00 � 0.9 � 0.8 = $10.80. The price is locked at that
229	moment and is never recalculated, so Happy Hour ending before payment does not change any bill.
230

349	5. Sequence diagram: a VIP orders a Burger during Happy Hour
350	
351	We picked a Stage 3 use case so that one scenario would show a Stage 2 behaviour (the VIP discount), a
352	Stage 3 feature (Happy Hour) and the whole flow from arrival to payment. The diagram is
353	docs/diagrams/stage-3-sequence.pdf (three pages, source stage-3-sequence.puml).
354	
355	Stage                  Stage 3, base setting (18 turns, 2 tables)
356	Starting conditions        Turn 1, restaurant empty, both tables free, Happy Hour unused. The
357	                           VIP arrives with satisfaction 100
358	
359	Manager decision           Switch Happy Hour on in turn 1 (y) or not (n)
360	
361	Outcome with y             Bill locked at $10.80; VIP pays on turn 5 with satisfaction 95
362	
363	Outcome with n             Bill $13.50; satisfaction 90 at payment
364	
365	Lifelines                  Manager (actor), main : Main, cli : RestaurantCLI (boundary),
366	                           game : GameController, staff : StaffActions, cmd :
367	                           ManagerCommand (control), schedule, restaurant, hh :
368	                           HappyHour, t1 : Table, host, waiter, chef, cashier, vip :
369	                           VIPCustomer, order : Order (entity)
370	
371	Walkthrough, turn by turn
372	
373	1. Turn 1. game asks schedule.isArrivalTurn(1), then createArrival(1) creates the
374	    VIPCustomer (WAITING), which restaurant.registerArrival adds to the queue. game checks
375	    hh.isAvailable() and cli asks the manager; on y, hh.activate() sets two remaining turns
376	    (an alt shows the n branch). The Host is left on WAIT, so the Waiter seats the VIP: cmd =
377	    parse(choice), staff.waiter(cmd, 1), then restaurant.seatFromQueue(vip, t1,
378	    waiter::seatCustomer), which calls waiter.seatCustomer(vip, t1); the Waiter then calls
379	    t1.seat(vip) and vip.markSeated(). The queue entry is removed only after seating
380	    succeeds. At the end of the turn the waiting loop calls vip.reduceSatisfaction(), which
381	    dispatches to the VIP's loss of 5 (100  95).
382	
383	2. Turn 2. Happy Hour recovery gives +15 to waiting customers (95  100). The Waiter takes the
384	    order: the VIP chooses the Burger, calculateBillCents returns $13.50 (VIP override),
385	    hh.applyDiscount makes it $10.80, and new Order(item, bill, turn) locks it. The Chef
386	    then calls order.advancePreparation(); one unit is enough, so the order goes PLACED 
387	    READY. The Waiter has already acted this turn, so serving waits for turn 3. End of turn: 100 
388	    95.
389	
390	3. Turn 3. The Waiter serves inside a loop until the choice is accepted. An alt shows the
391	    guard: a READY order is served (markServed, VIP  SERVED, servedTurn = 3); otherwise cmd
392	    throws "only a customer with a READY order can be served" and the manager is asked again
393	    without losing the task.
394	
395	4. Turn 4. Customer 2 arrives and the Host seats them. In step 4, vip.canFinishEating(4) is
396	    true, so the VIP becomes READY_TO_PAY (readyTurn = 4).
397	
398	5. Turn 5. restaurant finds the oldest eligible payer, cashier.processPayment(vip,
399	    restaurant) reads the locked getBillCents() (not recalculated), recordPayment adds $10.80
400	    to revenue, the VIP and order become PAID, and releaseTableOf(vip) frees t1.

404	
405	How the interactions implement the use case. The messages show two of our design decisions. The
406	controller never checks what type of customer it has; the VIP's smaller loss and its discount come from
407	overridden methods called on a Customer reference. The bill is also worked out only once, in
408	Waiter.takeOrder, and stored in Order. On turn 5 the Cashier just reads that amount, which is how
409	the price stays locked from the moment the order is taken.
410	
411	6. Design considerations
412	
413	6.1 Who owns what
414	
415	We tried to give every rule one clear owner. GameController only decides the order of the steps in a
416	turn; at 150 lines it no longer parses input or runs tasks itself. ManagerCommand reads what the manager
417	typed and works out which customer it refers to, and StaffActions turns that command into a call on
418	the Host, Waiter or Chef. Anything that differs between customer types lives in the Customer
419	subclasses. Billing is split on purpose: Waiter.takeOrder works out the price, Order keeps it and
420	Cashier only reads it. Restaurant owns the queue, the tables and the rules for who each staff member
421	can act on, so the screen and the controller always agree. RestaurantCLI only displays and reads input.
422	
423	Restaurant is the largest entity at about 230 lines, but most of that is small query methods over data it
424	already owns, so no single class ends up doing everything.
425	
426	6.2 OO concepts and SOLID, with code evidence
427	
428	    � Single Responsibility. The Stage 3 clean-up split the old controller into GameController,
429	         ManagerCommand and StaffActions, which took it from 324 to 150 lines.
430	
431	    � Open/Closed. Stage 2 added three customer types without editing the controller's turn logic.
432	         When Stage 3 added ComboMeal, Chef, Cashier, Menu and Order needed no change to handle
433	         it.
434	
435	    � Liskov Substitution. Any Customer subclass can be used wherever a Customer is expected, and
436	         the shared lifecycle guards still hold. A ComboMeal works anywhere a MenuItem does; a test puts
437	         a 3-unit combo in an ordinary Order and checks it is READY only after the third
438	         advancePreparation(), the same call the Chef makes.
439	
440	    � Dependency direction. The CLI no longer depends on the controller (Stage 3 clean-up). Main
441	         wires them together, and GameTests checks that the CLI source never mentions
442	         GameController.
443	
444	    � Encapsulation. Order and customer state change only through methods that check the current
445	         status. getWaitingQueue() returns a read-only view and seatFromQueue is the only way out of
446	         the queue.
447	
448	    � Polymorphism vs instanceof. GameTests reads the controller, StaffActions and
449	         ManagerCommand sources and fails if any of them contains instanceof or names a customer
450	         type.
451	
452	6.3 Trade-offs and alternatives we considered

538	7. Testing and results
539	
540	Every tag builds and passes its own checks: 55 at stage-1, 108 at stage-2, 194 at stage-3. GameTests
541	is plain Java with no test framework, so it builds and runs the same way as the game:
542	
543	javac --release 17 -d out -sourcepath src test/restaurantrush/GameTests.java
544	java -cp out restaurantrush.GameTests
545	
546	Reference values (all checked by GameTests):

568	
569	In the defeat run the only customer who pays has full satisfaction, which shows that meeting one target
570	is not enough: all three have to be met.
571	
572	Manager decisions that change the outcome (found by replaying the victory run with one choice
573	changed):
574	
575	     � Never switching Happy Hour on gives $86.40 revenue and 80.2 average satisfaction instead of
576	         $83.70 and 81.2. Happy Hour trades $2.70 of revenue for about one satisfaction point.
577	
578	     � On turn 13 the Waiter can either serve the Critic or take customer 5's order. Serving the Critic
579	         first gives it 54 satisfaction at payment. Doing it the other way round costs the Critic another 12
580	         (42 at payment) and drops the average from 81.2 to 78.8.
581	
582	Invalid input. The victory run includes a rejected Host choice (SEAT 5 on turn 1, before customer 5
583	exists) and a rejected Waiter choice (SERVE 3 on turn 6). Each prints the reason and asks again in the
584	same turn without using up the task. A bare SERVE or COOK picks the only eligible customer and is
585	refused when more than one qualifies.
586	
587	No double actions or payments. Each staff prompt accepts one valid task per turn. The Cashier charges
588	at most one customer per turn, and only one who became READY_TO_PAY on an earlier turn. Customer
589	and Order refuse to be marked PAID twice, and an abandoned customer's order is cancelled so it can
590	never be paid. GameTests checks that paying twice is rejected and that an abandoned customer's order
591	is cancelled and adds no revenue.
